Diagrams: logicprobe-uml; concurrency: logicprobe-concurrency; data: logicprobe-datamodel. ([AmethystLuna/logicprobe](https://github.com/AmethystLuna/logicprobe/tree/HEAD/skills/logicprobe) · ★5)
- **logicprobe-concurrency**: Use when a design or plan claims concurrency safety (thread-safe, lock-free, no data race, ISR-safe) or names shared state, mutexes, atomics or interrupts. - **logicprobe-datamodel**: Use when reviewing design docs, specs, plans or migrations claiming entities, fields, constraints, relationships, data invariants, or before/after data-model equivalence: schema changes, migration logic, or data guarantees ('all records must have X', 'no orphan rows', 'ids unique') want executable data-model verification (migration coverage, copy consistency, rollback symmetry, DD1-DD4 regression). - **logicprobe-structure**: Use when reviewing architecture, module structure or a dependency diagram: parse a component, package, class or deployment diagram into a dependency graph and check isolated nodes, dangling endpoints, cycles, allowed-dependency violations, layer violations and missing required edges. - **logicprobe-uml**: Use when drawing a code flow as UML or auditing a diagram someone drew: parse a hand-drawn state or activity diagram, render a model, and check round-trip fidelity (dead ends, unreachable states, label drift).
